# PM Transcripts (pmtranscripts.pmc.gov.au) - transcript ids for aus_pmtranscripts.yml.
#
# Each line is the XML API URL for one transcript. The human-facing page for id N is
#   https://pmtranscripts.pmc.gov.au/release/transcript-N
# The API is used instead because it carries title / prime-minister / date / release-type
# as single clean elements, where the HTML page has a site-banner h1 and no date element.
#
# 15333 ids, derived from the Internet Archive CDX index of /release/transcript-* on
# 2026-09-01 (15,455 captures -> 15,339 well-formed ids, 6 malformed dropped). The site
# states the collection holds ~26,000 items, so this list is ~59% of the archive; ids are
# NOT contiguous (they run 5..49,999 with 25,000-29,999 and 35,000-39,999 empty), which is
# why a sequential sweep was not used. Regenerate/extend with recipe_tools/cdx.py.
#
